### PART II: INORGANIC, ORGANIC & APPLIED CHEMISTRY
* **Ch 12 (Nature of Matter & Colloids):** Elements, compounds, mixtures. Colloids: Tyndall effect (light scattering), Brownian motion (kinetic stability). Lyophilic (reversible, stable) vs Lyophobic (irreversible, requires stabilizer). Emulsions: $O/W$ (milk) vs $W/O$ (butter). Hardy-Schulze Rule: Coagulating power increases with ionic valence ($\text{Al}^{3+} > \text{Mg}^{2+} > \text{Na}^+$).
* **Ch 13 (Atomic Structure & Quantum Numbers):** Thomson (plum pudding), Rutherford ($\alpha$-scattering, nucleus), Bohr ($mvr = \frac{nh}{2\pi}$). Quantum numbers: Principal $n$ (shell/energy), Azimuthal $l$ (subshell/shape: $s=0, p=1, d=2, f=3$), Magnetic $m_l$ (orbital orientation: $-l \dots +l$), Spin $m_s$ ($\pm \frac{1}{2}$). Pauli Exclusion: No two electrons have identical 4 quantum numbers. Hund's Rule: Maximum multiplicity. Aufbau Principle: Filling order $1s < 2s < 2p < 3s < 3p < 4s < 3d$. Isotopes (same $Z$, different $A$), Isobars (same $A$, different $Z$), Isotones (same neutrons $A - Z$).
* **Ch 14 (Chemical Bonding & Periodic Table):** Modern Periodic Law (Moseley): Properties are periodic functions of **Atomic Number ($Z$)**. Trends across period ($L \to R$): Atomic radius $\downarrow$, Ionization enthalpy $\uparrow$, Electronegativity $\uparrow$ (Fluorine highest $= 4.0$). Trends down group: Radius $\uparrow$, IE $\downarrow$, Metallic character $\uparrow$. Bonding: Ionic (electrostatic, high MP), Covalent (electron sharing), Coordinate (dative, Lewis acid-base adduct), Hydrogen bonding (dipole-dipole involving H with F, O, N; causes high boiling point of $\text{H}_2\text{O}$ and anomalous open ice cage structure).
* **Ch 15 (Chemical Reactions, Catalysis & Redox):** Types: Combination, Decomposition, Displacement, Double displacement. Oxidation: Loss of electrons / increase in oxidation number; Reduction: Gain of electrons / decrease in oxidation number. Catalysis: Lowers activation energy $E_a$ without altering equilibrium constant $K_{eq}$ or $\Delta G^\circ$.
* **Ch 16 (Acids, Bases, Salts & pH):** Arrhenius ($\text{H}^+$ / $\text{OH}^-$ in water), Brønsted-Lowry (proton donor / acceptor), Lewis (electron pair acceptor / donor). $\text{pH} = -\log_{10}[\text{H}^+]$; $\text{pH} + \text{pOH} = 14$ at $25^\circ\text{C}$. Indicators: Litmus (acid red, base blue), Phenolphthalein (acid colorless, base pink), Methyl orange (acid red, base yellow). Important salts: Washing Soda ($\text{Na}_2\text{CO}_3 \cdot 10\text{H}_2\text{O}$), Baking Soda ($\text{NaHCO}_3$), Bleaching Powder ($\text{CaOCl}_2$), Plaster of Paris ($\text{CaSO}_4 \cdot \frac{1}{2}\text{H}_2\text{O}$; gypsum is $\text{CaSO}_4 \cdot 2\text{H}_2\text{O}$).
* **Ch 17 (Metals, Non-Metals & Metallurgy):** Reactivity series: $\text{K} > \text{Na} > \text{Ca} > \text{Mg} > \text{Al} > \text{Zn} > \text{Fe} > \text{Pb} > [\text{H}] > \text{Cu} > \text{Hg} > \text{Ag} > \text{Au}$. Ores: Bauxite ($\text{Al}_2\text{O}_3 \cdot 2\text{H}_2\text{O}$), Hematite ($\text{Fe}_2\text{O}_3$), Cinnabar ($\text{HgS}$), Galena ($\text{PbS}$). Concentration: Froth flotation for sulfide ores. Roasting (heating with excess air) vs Calcination (heating in absence of air). Key Alloys: Brass ($\text{Cu} + \text{Zn}$), Bronze ($\text{Cu} + \text{Sn}$), Solder ($\text{Pb} + \text{Sn}$), Stainless Steel ($\text{Fe} + \text{Cr} + \text{Ni} + \text{C}$), Duralumin ($\text{Al} + \text{Cu} + \text{Mg} + \text{Mn}$).
* **Ch 18 (Carbon & Hydrocarbons):** Catenation and tetravalency. Allotropes: Diamond ($sp^3$, tetrahedral 3D network, hardest, insulator), Graphite ($sp^2$, hexagonal planar sheets, delocalized electrons, conductor, solid lubricant), Fullerenes ($C_{60}$, buckyball, 12 pentagons + 20 hexagons), Graphene (single 2D layer). Saturated: Alkanes ($\text{C}_n\text{H}_{2n+2}$); Unsaturated: Alkenes ($\text{C}_n\text{H}_{2n}$), Alkynes ($\text{C}_n\text{H}_{2n-2}$), Aromatic (Benzene $\text{C}_6\text{H}_6$). CNG is predominantly Methane ($\text{CH}_4$); LPG is Propane + Butane (odorized with Ethyl Mercaptan $\text{C}_2\text{H}_5\text{SH}$).
* **Ch 19 (Chemistry in Everyday Life):** Soaps (sodium/potassium salts of long-chain fatty acids; fail in hard water containing $\text{Ca}^{2+}/\text{Mg}^{2+}$ forming scum) vs Synthetic Detergents (alkyl sulfates, work in hard water). Polymers: Thermoplastics (Polythene, PVC; remoldable) vs Thermosetting (Bakelite, Melamine; cross-linked, irreversible). Glass (amorphous supercooled liquid $\text{SiO}_2$). Cement: Portland cement ($60–67\%\text{ CaO}, 17–25\%\text{ SiO}_2$; Gypsum $\text{CaSO}_4 \cdot 2\text{H}_2\text{O}$ added to slow down setting time). Explosives: RDX, TNT, Nitroglycerin (dynamite).

---

### PART III: BIOLOGICAL SYSTEMS & PHYSIOLOGY
* **Ch 20 (Cell Biology & Division):** Cell Theory (Schleiden, Schwann; Virchow: *Omnis cellula e cellula*). Prokaryote (70S, nucleoid, peptidoglycan) vs Eukaryote (80S, membrane organelles). Fluid Mosaic Model: Phospholipid bilayer + cholesterol + intrinsic/extrinsic proteins. Endomembrane System: ER (RER protein, SER lipid/detox) $\to$ Golgi (glycosylation) $\to$ Lysosomes (acid hydrolases, pH 5) $\to$ Vacuoles. Semi-autonomous: Mitochondria (cristae, TCA, ATP) & Chloroplasts (thylakoids, RuBisCO, Calvin cycle). Cell cycle: $G_1 \to S$ (DNA doubles $2C \to 4C$, chromosome number stays $2n$) $\to G_2 \to M$. Mitosis (equational); Meiosis (reductional, Prophase I LZPDD: Zygotene synapsis, Pachytene crossing over, Diplotene chiasmata).
* **Ch 21 (Biomolecules & Enzymes):** Monosaccharides (Glucose, Fructose) vs Disaccharides (Maltose $\alpha1\to4$, Lactose $\beta1\to4$, Sucrose $\alpha1\leftrightarrow2\beta$ [non-reducing]). Polysaccharides: Starch (Amylose $\alpha1\to4$ blue with $\text{I}_2$; Amylopectin $\alpha1\to6$), Glycogen (animal starch), Cellulose ($\beta1\to4$, no color with $\text{I}_2$), Chitin (NAG polymer). Amino acids: Zwitterion at pI; essential (PVT TIM HaLL). Proteins: $1^\circ$ (peptide), $2^\circ$ ($\alpha$-helix, $\beta$-sheet; H-bonds), $3^\circ$ (3D conformation, disulfide bridges), $4^\circ$ (multimeric, Hemoglobin $\alpha_2\beta_2$). Enzymes: Lower $E_a$; competitive inhibition ($V_{max}$ unchanged, $K_m \uparrow$), non-competitive ($V_{max} \downarrow$, $K_m$ unchanged). B-DNA: $2.0\text{ nm}$ diameter, $3.4\text{ nm}$ pitch, $10\text{ bp/turn}$, $0.34\text{ nm}$ rise; Chargaff: $\frac{A+G}{T+C} = 1.0$.
* **Ch 22 (Classification & Microorganisms):** 5 Kingdoms (Whittaker): Monera, Protista, Fungi, Plantae, Animalia. 3 Domains (Woese): Archaea, Bacteria, Eukarya. Monera: Archaebacteria (ether-linked branched lipids), Eubacteria (Gram-positive thick peptidoglycan vs Gram-negative LPS endotoxin), Mycoplasma (no cell wall, smallest, penicillin-resistant). Protista: Diatoms (silica shells), Dinoflagellates (red tide Gonyaulax, saxitoxin), Euglena (mixotrophic), Protozoans (Plasmodium). Fungi: Chitin cell wall; Lichens (alga + fungus, $\text{SO}_2$ bioindicator). Viruses (DNA OR RNA + capsid), Viroids (naked ssRNA), Prions (infectious misfolded protein only, BSE/CJD).
* **Ch 23 (Plant Physiology):** Water potential $\Psi_w = \Psi_s + \Psi_p$. Cohesion-tension-transpiration pull (Dixon & Joly). Stomata open via active $K^+$ influx (Levitt). Nitrogen fixation: Nitrogenase requires $16\text{ ATP}$ per $\text{N}_2$; Leghemoglobin oxygen scavenger. Photosynthesis: Light reactions (Z-scheme: PSII $P_{680} +$ PSI $P_{700} \to$ ATP, NADPH, $\text{O}_2$ via photolysis of $\text{H}_2\text{O}$); $C_3$ Calvin cycle (RuBisCO $\to$ 3-PGA; high photorespiration); $C_4$ Hatch-Slack (Kranz anatomy, PEPCase in mesophyll, zero photorespiration); CAM (scotoactive stomata open at night). Respiration: Glycolysis (cytosol, net 2 ATP + 2 NADH) $\to$ Link $\to$ TCA (matrix) $\to$ ETC (inner membrane); RQ: carbs = 1.0, fats = 0.7, proteins = 0.8–0.9. Phytohormones: Auxin (apical dominance, 2,4-D weedicide), Gibberellin (bolting, stem elongation), Cytokinin (cell division, anti-senescence), ABA (stress, stomatal closure), Ethylene (fruit ripening).
* **Ch 24 (Human Physiology I — Digestion, Respiration, Circulation):** Dental formula $\frac{2123}{2123} \times 2 = 32$; Enamel = Calcium hydroxyapatite. Stomach: Parietal cells (HCl + Castle's Intrinsic Factor for $\text{B}_{12}$), Chief cells (Pepsinogen). Liver: Bile (no enzymes, bile salts emulsify fats). Pancreas: Trypsinogen (activated by intestinal Enterokinase). Spirometry: $\text{TV} = 500\text{ mL}$, $\text{VC} = \text{TV} + \text{IRV} + \text{ERV} \approx 4600\text{ mL}$, $\text{TLC} = \text{VC} + \text{RV} \approx 5800\text{ mL}$ (RV cannot be measured by spirometer). Bohr effect: Right shift of $\text{Hb-O}_2$ curve (high $\text{CO}_2$, low pH, high temp, 2,3-BPG). $\text{CO}_2$ transport: $70\%$ as $\text{HCO}_3^-$ (Carbonic anhydrase + Chloride shift). Heart: SA Node ($72\text{ bpm}$) $\to$ AV Node $\to$ Bundle of His $\to$ Purkinje. Sounds: Lub (AV valves close), Dub (Semilunar close). $\text{Cardiac Output} = \text{SV } (70\text{ mL}) \times \text{HR } (72) \approx 5\text{ L/min}$. Blood: Universal Donor $O^-$, Recipient $AB^+$; Erythroblastosis fetalis ($\text{Rh}^-$ mother, $\text{Rh}^+$ fetus).
* **Ch 25 (Human Physiology II — Excretion, Nervous, Endocrine):** Nitrogen excretion: Ammonotelic (fish) vs Ureotelic (mammals, urea via liver ornithine cycle) vs Uricotelic (birds/reptiles, uric acid paste). Nephron: PCT (100% glucose reabsorption), Loop of Henle (counter-current multiplier, $1200\text{ mOsm/L}$), DCT (aldosterone $Na^+$ uptake, ADH water uptake). $\text{GFR} = 125\text{ mL/min} = 180\text{ L/day}$ ($99\%$ reabsorbed $\implies 1.5\text{ L/day}$ urine). RAAS: Renin $\to$ Angiotensin I $\to$ Angiotensin II $\to$ Aldosterone (ANF is antagonist). Nerve impulse: RMP $-70\text{ mV}$, Depolarization $+30\text{ mV}$ ($Na^+$ in), Repolarization ($K^+$ out). Brain: Cerebrum (cognition), Hypothalamus (thermostat, hunger, thirst), Cerebellum (balance, coordination), Medulla (cardiovascular, respiratory, vomiting). Endocrine: Anterior pituitary (GH, TSH, ACTH, LH, FSH, PRL), Posterior (Oxytocin, ADH). Thyroid ($\text{T}_3/\text{T}_4$, BMR; Calcitonin lowers $\text{Ca}^{2+}$), Parathyroid (PTH elevates $\text{Ca}^{2+}$). Pancreas (Insulin lowers glucose, Glucagon raises).
* **Ch 26 (Genetics, Molecular Biology & Reproduction):** Menstrual cycle: Day 14 LH surge triggers ovulation; Corpus Luteum secretes progesterone; Placental hCG maintains pregnancy. Mendel: Monohybrid $3:1$ ($1:2:1$), Dihybrid $9:3:3:1$. Linkage (Morgan, Drosophila, centimorgans). Bird sex determination: Female ZW, Male ZZ; Honeybees: Haplodiploidy. Central Dogma: Replication (semi-conservative, Meselson-Stahl $^{15}\text{N}/^{14}\text{N}$; DNA Pol III, Ligase), Transcription (RNA Pol II makes mRNA; $5'$ Cap, $3'$ Poly-A, Splicing), Genetic Code (61 sense, 3 stop UAA/UAG/UGA, AUG start/Met), Lac Operon (negative inducible, allolactose inducer). Disorders: Sickle Cell ($\text{Glu6Val}$ point mutation, $\beta$-chain), Down ($47, +21$), Turner ($45, X0$), Klinefelter ($47, XXY$).
* **Ch 27 (Health, Diseases, Immunology & Biotechnology):** Bacterial: Typhoid (Widal test), TB (Mantoux, BCG, DOTS), Tetanus (Tetanospasmin neurotoxin). Viral: Dengue (Aedes, thrombocytopenia), Polio (anterior horn destruction, OPV Sabin vs IPV Salk), Rabies (Negri bodies, hydrophobia), AIDS (HIV retrovirus, CD4+ T-cells $<200$, ELISA + Western Blot). Protozoan: Malaria (*P. falciparum*, female Anopheles, Hemozoin chills, fertilization in mosquito gut). Immunity: Innate (skin, HCl, lysozyme, interferons for viruses) vs Acquired: Humoral (B-cells $\to$ antibodies) vs Cell-Mediated (T-cells $\to$ graft rejection, suppressed by Cyclosporin A). Antibodies ($H_2L_2$): IgG (crosses placenta), IgA (colostrum), IgM (pentamer, primary response), IgE (allergy, mast cells). Biotech: Humulin (Eli Lilly, 1983, separate A & B chains, NO C-peptide), Gene therapy (1990: ADA-SCID using retrovirus), Bt Cotton (*Bacillus thuringiensis*, Cry endotoxins activated by alkaline insect gut), RNAi (*Meloidogyne incognita* nematode silenced by dsRNA).

---cf:Te8b,| Concept A | Concept B | Definitive Epistemic Criterion / Scientific Distinction |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Mass** | **Weight** | Mass is an intrinsic scalar property ($kg$, invariant everywhere); Weight is a gravitational force vector ($\vec{W} = m\vec{g}$, Newtons, zero at Earth's center and in free-fall). |
| **Heat** | **Temperature** | Heat is total thermal energy transferred ($\text{Joules}$); Temperature is average translational kinetic energy of molecules ($\text{Kelvin}$). |
| **Evaporation** | **Boiling** | Evaporation is a spontaneous surface phenomenon at *any* temperature; Boiling is a bulk phenomenon occurring strictly at the temperature where vapor pressure equals external atmospheric pressure. |
| **Real Image** | **Virtual Image** | Real images are formed by actual converging light rays and can be projected onto a screen (inverted); Virtual images are formed by diverging rays appearing to diverge from a point behind the optic and cannot be caught on a screen (erect). |
| **Alpha ($\alpha$) Rays** | **Beta ($\beta$) Rays** | $\alpha$-particles are heavy helium nuclei ($^4_2\text{He}^{2+}$, high ionizing power, low penetration, stopped by paper); $\beta$-particles are high-speed electrons ($^0_{-1}e$, moderate ionization, high penetration, stopped by aluminum foil). |
| **Diamond** | **Graphite** | Diamond has $sp^3$ tetrahedral 3D covalent lattice (hardest natural substance, electrical insulator); Graphite has $sp^2$ planar hexagonal sheets with delocalized $\pi$-electrons (soft, slippery solid lubricant, excellent electrical conductor). |
| **Exothermic** | **Endothermic** | Exothermic releases heat ($\Delta H < 0$, products more stable, e.g., combustion, cellular respiration); Endothermic absorbs heat ($\Delta H > 0$, e.g., photosynthesis, thermal decomposition of $\text{CaCO}_3$). |
| **$C_3$ Photosynthesis** | **$C_4$ Photosynthesis** | $C_3$ fixes $\text{CO}_2$ via RuBisCO into 3-PGA; suffers massive photorespiration waste at high temps. $C_4$ has Kranz anatomy, fixes $\text{CO}_2$ via PEPCase into OAA in mesophyll cells, releasing it in bundle sheath cells; zero photorespiration, maximum tropical efficiency. |
| **Mitosis** | **Meiosis** | Mitosis: Single equational division yielding 2 diploid ($2n$) genetically identical clones; Meiosis: Two divisions yielding 4 haploid ($n$) genetically diverse gametes via synapsis and crossing over. |
| **DNA** | **RNA** | DNA has 2-deoxyribose, thymine, double helix, highly stable genetic archive; RNA has reactive $2'\text{-OH}$ ribose, uracil, single-stranded, chemically labile, catalytic ribozymes. |
| **Active Transport** | **Facilitated Diffusion** | Active transport pumps solutes *against* electrochemical gradient ($\Delta G > 0$) requiring ATP or ion gradients; Facilitated diffusion moves solutes *down* gradient ($\Delta G < 0$) using carrier/channel proteins without cellular energy expenditure. |
| **Gram-Positive** | **Gram-Negative** | Gram-positive: Thick peptidoglycan layer, teichoic acids, stains purple, highly penicillin-susceptible; Gram-negative: Thin peptidoglycan, outer lipopolysaccharide (LPS) endotoxin membrane, stains pink, resistant to penicillin. |
| **Humoral Immunity** | **Cell-Mediated Immunity** | Humoral: B-lymphocytes synthesize circulating antibodies neutralizing extracellular pathogens; Cell-Mediated: T-lymphocytes (CD8+ cytotoxic) destroy intracellular infected/cancer cells and mediate allograft organ rejection. |
| **Type 1 Diabetes** | **Type 2 Diabetes** | Type 1: Autoimmune destruction of pancreatic $\beta$-cells causing absolute insulin deficiency; Type 2: Peripheral insulin receptor resistance with relative secretory deficiency (lifestyle/metabolic). |

---d0:T2ddb,1. **Acceleration at Maximum Height of Projectile:** At the apex of vertical projectile motion, velocity is instantaneous zero ($v = 0$), but acceleration is **NOT zero**; it remains strictly **$g = 9.8\text{ m/s}^2$ downward**!
2. **Acceleration due to Gravity at Earth's Center:** Inside a solid sphere of uniform density, gravity drops linearly with depth; at the geometric center of the Earth, **$g = 0\text{ m/s}^2$** (a body is weightless, but its mass remains unchanged).
3. **Weightlessness in an Orbiting Satellite:** Astronauts float not because gravity is zero in space ($g$ at $400\text{ km}$ is still $\sim 90\%$ of surface gravity), but because the spacecraft and astronauts are in continuous, synchronized **free-fall toward Earth**!
4. **Centripetal Force Work Done:** In uniform circular motion, centripetal force is perpetually perpendicular to instantaneous displacement ($\theta = 90^\circ$). The work done is **strictly ZERO** ($W = F s \cos 90^\circ = 0$).
5. **Sound Propagation in Vacuum:** Light travels fastest in a vacuum ($3 \times 10^8\text{ m/s}$), but **sound CANNOT propagate in a vacuum** because it is a mechanical pressure wave requiring an elastic material medium.
6. **Twinkling of Stars vs. Planets:** Stars twinkle due to atmospheric refraction of pinpoint light sources; planets do not twinkle because they subtend larger angular discs that act as an array of point sources whose fluctuations average out.
7. **Blue Sky vs. Red Sunset:** Both are caused by **Rayleigh Scattering ($I \propto 1/\lambda^4$)**, NOT dispersion! Blue light scatters most during midday; at sunset, light traverses a much thicker atmospheric path, scattering out all short blue wavelengths so only long red wavelengths reach the eye.
8. **Apparent Depth of Water:** A pool of water appears shallower than its actual depth due to refraction ($d_{apparent} = \frac{d_{real}}{n}$).
9. **Rainbow Formation:** A primary rainbow is produced by **Refraction + Dispersion + Internal Reflection (at back of drop) + Refraction**, NOT total internal reflection alone!
10. **Convex vs. Concave Lens for Hypermetropia:** Hypermetropia (farsightedness) forms the image behind the retina; corrected by a **Convex (converging) lens**. Myopia (nearsightedness) forms the image in front of the retina; corrected by a **Concave (diverging) lens**.
11. **Electric Current Direction:** By convention, electric current flows from positive to negative terminal (direction of positive charge flow); electrons flow in the **exact opposite direction** (from negative to positive terminal).
12. **Household Appliance Wiring:** All domestic household appliances are connected in **Parallel**, NOT in series, so that each appliance receives the full $220\text{ V}$ line voltage and operating one does not break the circuit for others.
13. **Transformer Working Principle:** Transformers operate strictly on **Alternating Current (AC)** via mutual induction; if connected to Direct Current (DC), the primary coil burns out due to zero back-EMF and excessive current.
14. **Photoelectric Effect Photon Energy:** The kinetic energy of emitted photoelectrons depends strictly on the **frequency (or wavelength)** of incident light, NOT on light intensity! Light intensity governs the number of electrons emitted (photocurrent), not their energy.
15. **Semiconductor Resistance with Temperature:** Unlike metals (where resistance increases with temperature, $\alpha > 0$), in semiconductors (silicon, germanium), resistance **decreases exponentially with temperature ($\alpha < 0$)** because covalent bonds break, generating free electron-hole pairs.
16. **Rusting of Iron Mass Change:** When iron rusts ($4\text{Fe} + 3\text{O}_2 + x\text{H}_2\text{O} \to 2\text{Fe}_2\text{O}_3 \cdot x\text{H}_2\text{O}$), the total mass of the object **INCREASES** because oxygen and chemically bound water molecules are incorporated into the rust compound.
17. **Dry Ice Composition:** Dry ice is **Solid Carbon Dioxide ($\text{CO}_2$)**, NOT frozen water! Sublimes directly into gas at $-78.5^\circ\text{C}$ at atmospheric pressure without melting into liquid.
18. **Conductivity of Diamond vs. Graphite:** Diamond is the purest form of carbon but is an **electrical insulator** (all 4 valence electrons localized in $sp^3$ $\sigma$-bonds); Graphite is a good conductor because of delocalized $\pi$-electrons in its $sp^2$ sheet lattice.
19. **Acid Dilution Golden Rule:** **ALWAYS add Acid slowly to Water** with constant stirring; NEVER add water to concentrated acid! The hydration of acid is violently exothermic; adding water generates localized boiling that splatters concentrated corrosive acid.
20. **Tooth Decay pH Threshold:** Tooth enamel (calcium hydroxyapatite) begins to demineralize and corrode when oral **$\text{pH}$ drops below 5.5**, caused by bacterial fermentation of dietary sugars into lactic acid.
21. **Gas Responsible for Bhopal Disaster:** The lethal gas leaked during the 1984 Union Carbide disaster in Bhopal was **Methyl Isocyanate ($\text{CH}_3\text{NCO}$)**, NOT methyl isocyanide!
22. **LPG Odorant:** Liquefied Petroleum Gas (Propane + Butane) is naturally completely odorless; the powerful stench added to detect leaks is **Ethyl Mercaptan ($\text{C}_2\text{H}_5\text{SH}$)**.
23. **Plaster of Paris Formula:** PoP is Calcium Sulfate Hemihydrate: **$\text{CaSO}_4 \cdot \frac{1}{2}\text{H}_2\text{O}$** (or $2\text{CaSO}_4 \cdot \text{H}_2\text{O}$); Gypsum has two waters of crystallization: $\text{CaSO}_4 \cdot 2\text{H}_2\text{O}$.
24. **Hard Water Soap Precipitation:** Hard water containing dissolved $\text{Ca}^{2+}$ and $\text{Mg}^{2+}$ ions precipitates soaps as insoluble calcium/magnesium stearate (**Scum**); synthetic detergents do not form scum because their sulfonate salts are water-soluble.
25. **Reducing vs. Non-Reducing Sugar Trap:** Fructose is a ketohexose but is a **reducing sugar** (reduces Benedict's solution due to enediol isomerization in alkali); **Sucrose is strictly NON-REDUCING** because the glycosidic bond joins both anomeric carbons.
26. **Cellulose Iodine Test:** Starch turns deep blue with iodine because its amylose helix traps $\text{I}_3^-$ ions; **Cellulose gives NO color with iodine** because its rigid unbranched $\beta(1\to4)$ chains cannot form helical coils.
27. **Universal Genetic Code Exceptions:** The genetic code is nearly universal, but exceptions exist: in mammalian mitochondria, **UGA codes for Tryptophan** (not a stop codon), and **AGA/AGG are stop codons** (not Arginine).
28. **Amino Acid Chirality:** All 19 standard amino acids are chiral (optically active L-isomers); **Glycine ($\text{H}_2\text{N}-\text{CH}_2-\text{COOH}$) is strictly ACHIRAL** because its $\alpha$-carbon is bonded to two identical hydrogen atoms.
29. **Enzyme Kinetics with Competitive Inhibitors:** A competitive inhibitor **increases $K_m$** (reduces apparent substrate affinity), but **$V_{max}$ remains COMPLETELY UNCHANGED** because high substrate concentrations displace the inhibitor.
30. **Lipids in Tissue Fractionation:** Lipids have low molecular weights ($<800\text{ Da}$) and are chemically micromolecules, yet they separate into the **acid-insoluble macromolecular fraction** because their hydrophobic nature forms vesicular membrane fragments.
31. **Endomembrane System Exclusions:** Mitochondria, Chloroplasts, and Peroxisomes are **NEVER part of the endomembrane system** because their biogenesis and functions are independent of ER-Golgi vesicular transport.
32. **DNA vs. Chromosome Count in S-Phase:** During S-phase, DNA replicates from **$2C \to 4C$**, but the chromosome count remains strictly **$2n$** because sister chromatids remain attached at a single centromere.
33. **Centromere Division in Meiosis:** Centromeres **DO NOT split in Anaphase I** (homologous chromosome pairs separate); centromeres split exclusively during **Anaphase II** (and mitotic Anaphase).
34. **Smallest Free-Living Cells:** **Mycoplasmas (PPLO)** are the smallest known cells ($0.1 - 0.3\text{ }\mu\text{m}$); they **completely lack a cell wall** and are totally unaffected by Penicillin.
35. **Oxygen Origin in Photosynthesis:** The molecular oxygen ($\text{O}_2$) released during photosynthesis originates exclusively from the **Photolysis of Water ($\text{H}_2\text{O}$)**, NOT from carbon dioxide!
36. **Water-Use Efficiency: $C_4$ vs. $C_3$:** $C_4$ plants lose only half as much water as $C_3$ plants for the same amount of $\text{CO}_2$ fixed, exhibiting double the water-use efficiency.
37. **Plant Respiration Myth:** Plants do NOT "respire only at night"; **cellular respiration occurs continuously 24 hours a day** in all living plant cells. During the day, photosynthetic $\text{O}_2$ evolution exceeds respiratory $\text{O}_2$ consumption, masking respiration.
38. **Respiratory Quotient for Anaerobic Respiration:** When fermentation occurs in the complete absence of oxygen, $\text{O}_2 \text{ consumed} = 0 \implies \mathbf{\text{RQ} = \frac{\text{CO}_2}{0} = \infty\text{ (Infinity)}}$.
39. **Guttation vs. Dew:** Dew is atmospheric water vapor condensing on cold plant surfaces; Guttation is **liquid xylem sap forced out of marginal hydathodes** by high positive root pressure on humid mornings (contains dissolved salts).
40. **Bile Juice Enzymatic Content:** Bile juice produced by the liver contains **ZERO digestive enzymes**! It digests fats purely mechanically via bile salt emulsification and micelle formation.
41. **Enamel Hardness:** Tooth enamel is the hardest substance in the human body, composed of crystalline **Calcium Hydroxyapatite**, derived embryologically from **Ectoderm** (ameloblasts).
42. **Lung Volume Spirometry Limitation:** **Residual Volume (RV)** and **Functional Residual Capacity (FRC)** CANNOT be measured by standard spirometry because residual volume air cannot be exhaled.
43. **Carbon Monoxide Toxicity Mechanism:** CO causes asphyxiation not because it damages lung tissue, but because it binds hemoglobin with **$>200\text{ times}$ higher affinity than oxygen**, forming stable Carboxyhemoglobin and blocking $O_2$ transport.
44. **Universal Blood Donor & Recipient Precision:**
    * Universal Donor: Strictly **$O\text{ Negative } (O^-)$**, NOT $O^+$ (due to absence of A, B, and Rh antigens).
    * Universal Recipient: Strictly **$AB\text{ Positive } (AB^+)$**, NOT $AB^-$ (due to absence of anti-A, anti-B, and anti-Rh antibodies).
45. **Urea Synthesis Organ:** Urea is synthesized **EXCLUSIVELY in the Liver** via the Krebs-Henseleit Ornithine cycle; the kidneys only filter and excrete it!
46. **Diabetes Mellitus vs. Diabetes Insipidus:** Both present with polyuria and polydipsia, but:
    * Diabetes Mellitus: Insulin deficiency $\implies$ Hyperglycemia + Glucosuria.
    * Diabetes Insipidus: **ADH deficiency** $\implies$ Normal blood sugar, zero urine glucose, hypotonic dilute urine ($15\text{ L/day}$).
47. **Organ Transplant Graft Rejection:** Graft rejection is orchestrated predominantly by **Cell-Mediated Immunity (T-Lymphocytes)**, NOT humoral antibodies!
48. **Antibody Crossing Placenta:** **$\text{IgG}$ is the ONLY antibody class** capable of crossing the maternal placenta into fetal circulation. Colostrum contains secretory **$\text{IgA}$**.
49. **Recombinant Humulin Structure:** Genetically engineered Humulin (Eli Lilly) contains **NO C-peptide**! Chains A and B were produced separately in *E. coli* and joined chemically in vitro via disulfide bonds.
50. **Bt Toxin Gut Specificity:** Bt toxin does not harm humans or non-target insects because the crystalline protoxin requires the **alkaline pH of the insect midgut** for solubilization and cleavage into its active lethal pore-forming state.

---d1:Te62,### Question 1 (Cross-Domain Physics & Thermodynamics)
A metal sphere of radius $R$ at temperature $T_1$ cools down in an evacuated chamber maintained at temperature $T_0$. According to the Stefan-Boltzmann law, the initial net rate of heat loss from the sphere is proportional to:
A) $R \cdot (T_1 - T_0)$  
B) $R^2 \cdot (T_1^4 - T_0^4)$  
C) $R^3 \cdot (T_1^4 - T_0^4)$  
D) $R^2 \cdot (T_1 - T_0)^4$  

**Analytical Answer:**
* Stefan-Boltzmann law dictates that net radiated power is:
  $$\frac{dQ}{dt} = e \sigma A (T_1^4 - T_0^4)$$
* For a sphere of radius $R$, surface area $A = 4\pi R^2 \propto R^2$.
* Substituting area yields: $\frac{dQ}{dt} \propto R^2 (T_1^4 - T_0^4)$.
* **Correct Option:** **B) $R^2 \cdot (T_1^4 - T_0^4)$**.

### Question 2 (Chemical Dynamics & Coordination Bonding)
Consider the following statements regarding hardness of water and its removal:
1. Temporary hardness of water is caused by dissolved bicarbonates of calcium and magnesium and can be eliminated simply by boiling.
2. Permanent hardness of water is caused by chlorides and sulfates of calcium and magnesium and can be removed by treating with washing soda ($\text{Na}_2\text{CO}_3$).
3. The synthetic ion-exchange resin method removes both temporary and permanent hardness by exchanging $\text{Ca}^{2+}$ and $\text{Mg}^{2+}$ ions with $\text{H}^+$ or $\text{Na}^+$ ions.
4. Calgon process removes permanent hardness by forming an insoluble precipitate of calcium phosphate.

Which of the statements given above are correct?
A) 1 and 2 only  
B) 1, 2 and 3 only  
C) 2, 3 and 4 only  
D) 1, 3 and 4 only  

**Analytical Answer:**
* Statement 1 is **CORRECT**: Boiling decomposes soluble bicarbonates into insoluble carbonates:
  $$\text{Ca}(\text{HCO}_3)_2 \xrightarrow{\Delta} \text{CaCO}_3 \downarrow + \text{H}_2\text{O} + \text{CO}_2$$
* Statement 2 is **CORRECT**: Washing soda precipitates sulfates/chlorides as insoluble carbonates:
  $$\text{CaCl}_2 + \text{Na}_2\text{CO}_3 \longrightarrow \text{CaCO}_3 \downarrow + 2\text{NaCl}$$
* Statement 3 is **CORRECT**: Synthetic zeolites and cation/anion exchange resins replace multivalent divalent cations with monovalent sodium or hydronium ions.
* Statement 4 is **INCORRECT**: Calgon (Sodium hexametaphosphate, $\text{Na}_6\text{P}_6\text{O}_{18}$) does NOT form an insoluble precipitate; it forms a **highly soluble complex anion** entrapping $\text{Ca}^{2+}$:
  $$\text{Na}_2[\text{Na}_4(\text{PO}_3)_6] + 2\text{Ca}^{2+} \longrightarrow \text{Na}_2[\text{Ca}_2(\text{PO}_3)_6] + 4\text{Na}^+$$
* **Correct Option:** **B) 1, 2 and 3 only**.

### Question 3 (Advanced Epistemic Physiology & Genetics)
Which of the following biological processes does NOT involve the consumption of ATP?
A) Conversion of Glucose to Glucose-6-phosphate during Glycolysis  
B) Movement of water through root endodermis via the Casparian strip  
C) Transport of Sodium and Potassium ions via the axonal $Na^+/K^+$ ATPase pump  
D) Reduction of atmospheric Dinitrogen ($\text{N}_2$) to Ammonia by Nitrogenase  

**Analytical Answer:**
* Conversion of glucose to G-6-P consumes $1\text{ ATP}$ via Hexokinase $\implies$ *Consumes ATP*.
* Water movement across the root endodermis into xylem vessels is driven by **osmosis and transpirational suction pull** (physical water potential gradient), which does **NOT directly consume ATP**!
* $Na^+/K^+$ pump is primary active transport consuming $1\text{ ATP}$ per cycle $\implies$ *Consumes ATP*.
* Nitrogenase requires $16\text{ ATP}$ per molecule of $\text{N}_2$ fixed $\implies$ *Consumes massive ATP*.
* **Correct Option:** **B) Movement of water through root endodermis via the Casparian strip**.
